		    <script>
    // --- SEGURIDAD: Sanitización ---
    function escapeHTML(text) {
        if (!text) return "";
        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.textContent = text;
        return div.innerHTML;
    }

    // --- Lógica de Cookies ---
    function setCookie(name, v, d) { 
        let e = ""; 
        if (d) { 
            let date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime() + (400 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)); 
            e = "; expires=" + date.toUTCString(); 
            document.cookie = name + "=" + (v || "") + e + "; path=/; SameSite=Lax"; 
        } 
    }

    function getCookie(n) { 
        let ne = n + "="; 
        let ca = document.cookie.split(';'); 
        for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) { 
            let c = ca[i]; 
            while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1); 
            if (c.indexOf(ne) == 0) return c.substring(ne.length, c.length); 
        } 
        return null; 
    }

    // --- MEJORA: Renderizado Asíncrono (Evita saturar CPU) ---
    function renderizarListaFavoritos() {
        const container = document.getElementById('teleonline-lista-render');
        if (!container) return;

        let favs = getCookie('teleonline_favs') ? JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs')) : [];
        
        if (favs.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<p class="lista-favoritos-vacia">Todavía no has guardado ningún canal.</p>';
            return;
        }

        const params = new URLSearchParams();
        params.append('action', 'get_favs_data');
        params.append('ids', JSON.stringify(favs));

        fetch('https://teleonline.org/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
            body: params
        })
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(res => {
            if (!res.success || res.data.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p class="lista-favoritos-vacia">Todavía no has guardado ningún canal.</p>';
                return;
            }
            let html = '';
            res.data.forEach(post => {
                html += `
                <article class="article-loop asap-columns-5 fav-card-${post.id}" id="post-${post.id}" data-post-id="${post.id}">
                    <div class="fav-checkbox-wrapper"><input type="checkbox" class="fav-checkbox" value="${post.id}"></div>
                    <div class="fav-move-handle"><i class="ri-drag-move-2-fill"></i></div>
                    <a href="${post.url}">
                        <div class="article-content"><div style="background-image: url('${post.img}');" class="article-image"></div></div>
                        <p class="entry-title notranslate">${escapeHTML(post.title)}</p>
                    </a>
                </article>`;
            });
            container.innerHTML = html;
            // Si la barra de acciones estaba oculta y hay favs, se puede mostrar aquí si se requiere
        });
    }

    function refrescarCaducidadCookie() {
        let actuales = getCookie('teleonline_favs');
        if (actuales) { setCookie('teleonline_favs', actuales, 400); }
    }
    
    function verificarCookies() {
        if (!navigator.cookieEnabled) {
            const info = document.querySelector('.fav-info-discreto');
            if (info) {
                info.innerHTML = '<strong style="color:#ff4757;">Atención:</strong> Tienes las cookies desactivadas...';
                info.style.cssText = "color:#fff; background:rgba(255,71,87,0.15); padding:15px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #ff4757; margin-top:20px;";
            }
        }
    }

    // --- Funciones de Interfaz ---
    function crearParticulas(target) {
        const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
        const container = document.body;
        for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            const particle = document.createElement('i');
            particle.className = 'ri-heart-fill fav-particle';
            const x = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
            const y = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
            particle.style.left = x + 'px'; particle.style.top = y + 'px';
            const dX = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100; const dY = -Math.random() * 100 - 20;
            particle.style.setProperty('--x', `${dX}px`); particle.style.setProperty('--y', `${dY}px`);
            container.appendChild(particle);
            setTimeout(() => particle.remove(), 800);
        }
    }

    function mostrarNotificacion(mensaje) {
        const toast = document.createElement('div');
        toast.className = 'fav-toast';
        toast.innerHTML = `<i class="ri-checkbox-circle-fill"></i> ${escapeHTML(mensaje)}`;
        document.body.appendChild(toast);
        setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add('is-show'), 100);
        setTimeout(() => { toast.classList.remove('is-show'); setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 300); }, 3000);
    }

    function abrirModalFav(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.add('is-visible'); }
    function cerrarModalFav(id) { 
        document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('is-visible'); 
        if(id === 'modal-importar') document.getElementById('fav-import-input').value = '';
    }

    function exportarLista() {
        let favs = getCookie('teleonline_favs');
        if (!favs || favs === '[]') return alert('Tu lista está vacía');
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(btoa(favs)).then(() => mostrarNotificacion('Código copiado'));
    }

    function procesarImportacion() {
        const input = document.getElementById('fav-import-input');
        const code = input.value.trim();
        if (!code) return;
        try {
            const decoded = atob(code);
            const nuevosFavs = JSON.parse(decoded);
            if (Array.isArray(nuevosFavs)) {
                const idsValidos = nuevosFavs.map(id => parseInt(id)).filter(id => !isNaN(id)).slice(0, 100);
                let actuales = getCookie('teleonline_favs') ? JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs')) : [];
                const unicosNuevos = idsValidos.filter(id => !actuales.includes(id));
                if (unicosNuevos.length === 0) { alert("Los canales ya están en tu lista."); cerrarModalFav('modal-importar'); return; }
                cerrarModalFav('modal-importar');
                const modalConfirm = document.getElementById('modal-confirm-import');
                modalConfirm.querySelector('.count-text').innerText = unicosNuevos.length;
                document.getElementById('btn-confirm-merge').onclick = function() {
                    const listaFusionada = [...new Set([...actuales, ...idsValidos])].slice(0, 100);
                    setCookie('teleonline_favs', JSON.stringify(listaFusionada), 400);
                    location.reload();
                };
                abrirModalFav('modal-confirm-import');
            }
        } catch (e) { alert("Código no válido."); }
    }

    function confirmarVaciar() {
        setCookie('teleonline_favs', JSON.stringify([]), 400);
        location.reload();
    }

    function actualizarNotificacionMenu() {
        const menuItem = document.getElementById('menu-item-21313');
        if (!menuItem) return;
        let favs = getCookie('teleonline_favs') ? JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs')) : [];
        let badge = menuItem.querySelector('.fav-menu-badge');
        if (favs.length > 0) {
            if (!badge) { 
                badge = document.createElement('span'); badge.className = 'fav-menu-badge'; 
                menuItem.querySelector('a').appendChild(badge); 
            }
            badge.textContent = (favs.length >= 1000) ? (favs.length / 1000).toFixed(1) + 'k' : favs.length;
        } else if (badge) { badge.remove(); }
    }

    function actualizarEstadoBotonesFavoritos() {
        let favoritos = getCookie('teleonline_favs') ? JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs')) : [];
        document.querySelectorAll('.boton-favorito-main').forEach(function(btn) {
            var match = btn.className.match(/btn-fav-logic-(\d+)/);
            if (!match) return;
            var postId = parseInt(match[1]);
            var icon = btn.querySelector('i');
            if (favoritos.includes(postId)) {
                btn.classList.add('is-active');
                if (icon) icon.className = 'ri-heart-fill';
            } else {
                btn.classList.remove('is-active');
                if (icon) icon.className = 'ri-heart-line';
            }
        });
    }

    // --- Ordenación y Táctil ---
    let dragElement = null;
    function handleDragStart(e) { if (!document.querySelector('.lista-favoritos-container').classList.contains('modo-edicion')) return; dragElement = this; this.style.opacity = '0.4'; }
    function handleDragOver(e) { if (e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault(); return false; }
    function handleDrop(e) {
        if (dragElement !== this) {
            const container = document.querySelector('.lista-favoritos-container');
            const allNodes = Array.from(container.children);
            if (allNodes.indexOf(dragElement) < allNodes.indexOf(this)) { this.after(dragElement); } else { this.before(dragElement); }
            guardarNuevoOrden(dragElement);
        }
        return false;
    }
    function handleDragEnd() { this.style.opacity = '1'; }

    function handleTouchMove(e) {
        if (!dragElement) return;
        e.preventDefault(); 
        const touch = e.touches[0];
        const target = document.elementFromPoint(touch.clientX, touch.clientY);
        const card = target ? target.closest('.article-loop') : null;
        if (card && card !== dragElement && card.parentNode === dragElement.parentNode) {
            const container = dragElement.parentNode;
            const children = Array.from(container.children);
            if (children.indexOf(dragElement) < children.indexOf(card)) { card.after(dragElement); } else { card.before(dragElement); }
        }
    }

    function guardarNuevoOrden(el) {
        const items = document.querySelectorAll('.lista-favoritos-container .article-loop');
        const nuevoOrden = Array.from(items).map(item => parseInt(item.getAttribute('data-post-id')));
        setCookie('teleonline_favs', JSON.stringify(nuevoOrden), 400);
        if(el) { el.classList.add('fav-save-flash'); setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('fav-save-flash'), 400); }
    }

    function toggleModoEdicion() {
        const container = document.querySelector('.lista-favoritos-container');
        const bulkBar = document.querySelector('.fav-bulk-actions');
        container.classList.toggle('modo-edicion');
        bulkBar.classList.toggle('is-visible');
        
        container.querySelectorAll('.article-loop').forEach(item => {
            if (container.classList.contains('modo-edicion')) {
                const handle = item.querySelector('.fav-move-handle');
                item.setAttribute('draggable', 'true');
                item.addEventListener('dragstart', handleDragStart);
                item.addEventListener('dragover', handleDragOver);
                item.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);
                item.addEventListener('dragend', handleDragEnd);
                handle.ontouchstart = (e) => { dragElement = item; item.style.opacity = '0.5'; };
                handle.ontouchmove = handleTouchMove;
                handle.ontouchend = () => { if(dragElement) guardarNuevoOrden(dragElement); dragElement = null; item.style.opacity = '1'; };
            } else {
                item.setAttribute('draggable', 'false');
                document.querySelectorAll('.fav-checkbox').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);
            }
        });
    }

    function eliminarSeleccionados() {
        const seleccionados = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fav-checkbox:checked')).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value));
        if (seleccionados.length === 0) return;
        if (confirm(`¿Eliminar ${seleccionados.length} canales?`)) {
            let favoritos = JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs') || "[]");
            favoritos = favoritos.filter(id => !seleccionados.includes(id));
            setCookie('teleonline_favs', JSON.stringify(favoritos), 400);
            location.reload();
        }
    }

    function gestionarFavorito(postId, element) {
        let favoritos = getCookie('teleonline_favs') ? JSON.parse(getCookie('teleonline_favs')) : [];
        const index = favoritos.indexOf(postId);
        if (index > -1) {
            favoritos.splice(index, 1);
            mostrarNotificacion('Eliminado de favoritos');
            document.querySelectorAll(`.btn-fav-logic-${postId}`).forEach(btn => {
                btn.classList.remove('is-active');
                btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-heart-line"></i>';
            });
            const card = document.querySelector(`.fav-card-${postId}`);
            if(card) { card.style.opacity = '0'; setTimeout(() => card.remove(), 400); }
        } else {
            if (favoritos.length >= 100) return alert("Límite alcanzado.");
            favoritos.push(postId);
            
            mostrarNotificacion('Añadido a favoritos');
            
            if(element) crearParticulas(element);
            document.querySelectorAll(`.btn-fav-logic-${postId}`).forEach(btn => {
                btn.classList.add('is-active');
                btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ri-heart-fill"></i>';
            });
        }
        setCookie('teleonline_favs', JSON.stringify(favoritos), 400);
        actualizarNotificacionMenu();
    }

    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        verificarCookies();
        refrescarCaducidadCookie();
        actualizarNotificacionMenu();
        actualizarEstadoBotonesFavoritos();
        renderizarListaFavoritos();
    });
    </script>

<script>
var currentProgrammeData = null;
// Detectamos la URL base de la API de forma dinámica
var apiUrlBase = "https://teleonline.org/wp-json/teleonline/v1/channel/";
var currentPostId = "39392";

// === Corrección de horas (+2h porque los timestamps vienen en UTC+0) ===
function addHours(timeStr, hours) {
    if (!timeStr) return timeStr;
    var parts = timeStr.split(':');
    var h = (parseInt(parts[0]) + hours) % 24;
    return String(h).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + parts[1];
}

// === Mostrar/ocultar el icono de EPG según haya o no programación disponible ===
function toggleEpgIcon(show) {
    var epgIcon = document.getElementById('epg-popup-icon');
    if (!epgIcon) return;
    epgIcon.classList.toggle('hidden', !show);
}

// === Inicializar Overlay ===
function initEPGOverlay() {
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!currentProgrammeData) {
      console.warn("EPG no disponible");
      return;
    }

    // Limpieza de overlays antiguos para evitar duplicados
    const existingOverlay = document.querySelector('.epg-overlay');
    if (existingOverlay) existingOverlay.remove();

    // Nueva lógica: próximos 4 programas y formato 24h
    let nextProgramsPreview = '';
    if (currentProgrammeData.futuros && currentProgrammeData.futuros.length > 0) {
        let nextFour = currentProgrammeData.futuros.slice(0, 4);
        nextProgramsPreview = `
            <div class="next-programs" style="position: absolute; right: 40px; bottom: 23px; text-align: left; font-size: 11px; color: #bbb; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; line-height: 1.5; z-index: 10;">
                <div style="font-weight: 700; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 2px; font-size: 12px;">Siguiente</div>
                ${nextFour.map(p => `<div style="max-width: 190px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">${p.start} - ${p.title}</div>`).join('')}
            </div>
        `;
    }

    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'epg-overlay';
    overlay.innerHTML = `
    <div class="close-btn" onclick="this.closest('.epg-overlay').classList.add('hide')">✕</div>
      <div class="inner">
        ${currentProgrammeData.image ? `<img src="${currentProgrammeData.image}" alt="Imagen">` : ``}
        <div class="info">
          <div class="estas-viendo" style="font-size: 13px; color: #ccc;">Estás viendo</div>
          <div class="title">${currentProgrammeData.title}</div>
          <div class="time">${currentProgrammeData.start} - ${currentProgrammeData.stop}</div>
          <div class="progress-container"><div class="progress-bar" style="width:${currentProgrammeData.progress}%;"></div></div>
          <div class="desc" style="display:none">
              <span class="short-text">${currentProgrammeData.description?.trim() || 'El programa no tiene descripción disponible.'}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${nextProgramsPreview}
      </div>
    `;
    document.querySelector('#tab-content').appendChild(overlay);

    // Limpieza de popups antiguos
    const existingPopup = document.querySelector('.epg-popup');
    if (existingPopup) existingPopup.remove();

    // Estructura moderna de Smart TV UI
    const popup = document.createElement('div');
    popup.className = 'epg-popup';
    popup.innerHTML = `
      <div class="epg-popup-content">
        <div>
          <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: row; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start;">
            <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column;">
              <span class="estas-viendo">Estás viendo</span>
              <h3>${currentProgrammeData.title}</h3>
            </div>
            <button class="close-modal-btn" onclick="this.closest('.epg-popup').classList.remove('active')">✕</button>
          </div>
          <p>
            ${currentProgrammeData.description?.trim() || 'El programa no tiene descripción disponible.'}
          </p>
        </div>
        ${currentProgrammeData.futuros && currentProgrammeData.futuros.length > 0 ? `
          <h4>Siguientes programas</h4>
          ${currentProgrammeData.futuros.map(prog => `
            <div class="epg-programme-item">
              ${prog.image ? `<img src="${prog.image}" alt="Imagen">` : ``}
              <div class="info">
                <h5>${prog.title}</h5>
                <div class="time">${prog.start} - ${prog.stop}</div>
                <p>${prog.description?.trim() || 'El programa no tiene descripción disponible.'}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          `).join('')}
        ` : ''}
        <div class="epg-disclaimer">
          <span>
          Para ver la guía de televisión completa <a href="https://teleonline.org/guia-tv/">pulsa aquí</a>. Algunas de las imágenes aquí mostradas se obtienen de forma ética a través<br>
          de <a href="https://www.movistarplus.es/programacion-tv" rel="nofollow noopener">movistarplus.es</a>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(popup);

    // Ocultar automáticamente
    let hideTimeout = setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.add('hide'), 5000);

    const reproContainer = document.getElementById('tab-content');
    if (window.matchMedia('(hover: hover)').matches) {
      reproContainer.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
        if (overlay.classList.contains('hide')) {
          overlay.classList.remove('hide');
          clearTimeout(hideTimeout);
          hideTimeout = setTimeout(() => overlay.classList.add('hide'), 5000);
        }
      });
    }

    // === Barra de progreso dinámica ===
    (function() {
      const totalSeconds = currentProgrammeData.duration;
      let elapsedSeconds = currentProgrammeData.elapsed;
      const progressBar = overlay.querySelector('.progress-bar');

      function updateProgressBar() {
        if (!progressBar) return;
        const progressPercentage = (elapsedSeconds / totalSeconds) * 100;
        progressBar.style.width = Math.min(progressPercentage, 100) + '%';

        if (elapsedSeconds < totalSeconds) {
          elapsedSeconds++;
          setTimeout(updateProgressBar, 1000);
        }
      }
      updateProgressBar();
    })();

    // === Programar actualización automática ===
    (function() {
      const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      const stopTimestamp = currentProgrammeData.stop_timestamp;
      const msUntilNext = (stopTimestamp - now) * 1000;

      if (msUntilNext > 0) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          console.log("Programa terminado, buscando actualización...");
          fetch(apiUrlBase + currentPostId)
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(response => {
                if (response.success && response.data.current) {
                    var current = response.data.current;
                    current.start = addHours(current.start, 2);
                    current.stop  = addHours(current.stop, 2);
                    var futuros = (response.data.futuros || []).map(function(p) {
                        p.start = addHours(p.start, 2);
                        p.stop  = addHours(p.stop, 2);
                        return p;
                    });
                    currentProgrammeData = Object.assign(current, { futuros: futuros });
                    toggleEpgIcon(true);
                    initEPGOverlay();
                } else {
                    toggleEpgIcon(false);
                }
            })
            .catch(err => console.error("Error EPG actualización:", err));
        }, msUntilNext + 2000);
      }
    })();

  }, 1000);
}

// === Llamada API inicial (REST API) ===
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  fetch(apiUrlBase + currentPostId)
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(response => {
        if (response.success && response.data.current) {
            var current = response.data.current;
            current.start = addHours(current.start, 2);
            current.stop  = addHours(current.stop, 2);
            var futuros = (response.data.futuros || []).map(function(p) {
                p.start = addHours(p.start, 2);
                p.stop  = addHours(p.stop, 2);
                return p;
            });
            currentProgrammeData = Object.assign(current, { futuros: futuros });
            toggleEpgIcon(true);
            initEPGOverlay();
        } else {
            console.warn("Sin datos EPG (API response):", response);
            toggleEpgIcon(false);
        }
    })
    .catch(err => {
        console.error("Error EPG API:", err);
        toggleEpgIcon(false);
    });
});
</script>

                        var NEEDS_NOREFERRER = false;
                        var nativeVideo = null;   // referencia global al <video> nativo
                        var nativeHls = null;     // referencia al objeto hls.js (si aplica)
                        
                        function buildTrackMenu(hls, container){
                          // Solo para hls.js (desktop). En nativo el menú lo pone el navegador.
                          if (!hls) return;
                        
                          function makeBar(){
                            var bar = document.createElement("div");
                            bar.style.cssText = "display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:6px;background:#111;";
                            return bar;
                          }
                          function makeBtn(label, onClick){
                            var b = document.createElement("button");
                            b.textContent = label;
                            b.style.cssText = "padding:4px 10px;border:1px solid #444;background:#222;color:#eee;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;";
                            b.onclick = onClick;
                            return b;
                          }
                        
                          hls.on(Hls.Events.MANIFEST_PARSED, function(){
                            // --- Audio ---
                            if (hls.audioTracks && hls.audioTracks.length > 1) {
                              var aBar = makeBar();
                              hls.audioTracks.forEach(function(t, idx){
                                aBar.appendChild(makeBtn("🔊 " + (t.name || t.lang || ("Audio "+idx)), function(){
                                  hls.audioTrack = idx;
                                }));
                              });
                              container.appendChild(aBar);
                            }
                            // --- Subtítulos ---
                            if (hls.subtitleTracks && hls.subtitleTracks.length > 0) {
                              var sBar = makeBar();
                              sBar.appendChild(makeBtn("CC Off", function(){ hls.subtitleTrack = -1; }));
                              hls.subtitleTracks.forEach(function(t, idx){
                                sBar.appendChild(makeBtn("CC " + (t.name || t.lang || idx), function(){
                                  hls.subtitleDisplay = true;
                                  hls.subtitleTrack = idx;
                                }));
                              });
                              container.appendChild(sBar);
                            }
                          });
                        }
                        
                        function createNativeHlsPlayer(url, container){
                          container.innerHTML = "";
                          var video = document.createElement("video");
                          video.controls = true; video.autoplay = true; video.muted = true; video.playsInline = true;
                          video.setAttribute("playsinline","");
                          video.style.width = "100%"; video.style.height = "auto";
                          video.style.backgroundColor = "#000"; video.style.minHeight = "215px";
                        
                          nativeVideo = video;
                          nativeHls = null;
                        
                          if (video.canPlayType("application/vnd.apple.mpegurl")) {
                            // Nativo: el navegador muestra audio/subtítulos en su propio menú (Safari/iOS/Android/WebView)
                            video.src = url;
                            container.appendChild(video);
                          } else if (window.Hls && Hls.isSupported()) {
                            // Fallback desktop (Firefox y navegadores sin HLS nativo)
                            var hls = new Hls({ backBufferLength: 7200 });
                            nativeHls = hls;
                            hls.loadSource(url);
                            hls.attachMedia(video);
                            container.appendChild(video);
                            buildTrackMenu(hls, container);

                            // Si hls.js falla de forma fatal (p.ej. bloqueo CORS por Origin en RTVE
                            // desde navegadores sin HLS nativo), mostramos un mensaje claro.
                            hls.on(Hls.Events.ERROR, function(evt, data){
                              if (data.fatal) {
                                container.innerHTML = "<div style=\"height:450px;padding:2em;text-align:center;color:#ccc;background:#0a0a0a;border-radius:12px;min-height:215px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;\">"
                                  + "<div style=\"font-size:17px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;margin-bottom:8px;\">Canal no disponible en este navegador</div>"
                                  + "<div style=\"font-size:14px;color:#888;\">Para verlo, usa Google Chrome, Brave, Safari o la aplicación de teleonline.</div>"
                                  + "</div>";
                              }
                            });
                          } else {
                            // Último recurso: intentar src directo igualmente
                            video.src = url;
                            container.appendChild(video);
                          }
                        }

                            function checkCodecSupport() {
                                if (typeof MediaSource === 'undefined') return 'no-mse';
                                if (!MediaSource.isTypeSupported('audio/mp4; codecs="mp4a.40.2"')) return 'no-aac';
                                if (!MediaSource.isTypeSupported('video/mp4; codecs="avc1.42E01E"')) return 'no-h264';
                                return 'ok';
                            }

                            function createFallbackPlayer(url, container) {
                                container.innerHTML = '';

                                // Intento 1: video nativo directo (Safari, algunos Firefox)
                                var video = document.createElement('video');
                                video.controls = true;
                                video.autoplay = true;
                                video.muted = true;
                                video.playsInline = true;
                                video.style.width = '100%';
                                video.style.height = 'auto';
                                video.style.backgroundColor = '#000';
                                video.style.minHeight = '215px';

                                var source = document.createElement('source');
                                source.src = url;
                                source.type = 'application/vnd.apple.mpegurl';
                                video.appendChild(source);

                                // Si el video nativo también falla, mostrar opciones
                                video.addEventListener('error', function() {
                                    showFallbackMessage(url, container);
                                });

                                // Timeout: si en 8s no tiene datos, asumir que no funciona
                                var fallbackTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                                    if (video.readyState < 2) {
                                        showFallbackMessage(url, container);
                                    }
                                }, 8000);

                                video.addEventListener('canplay', function() {
                                    clearTimeout(fallbackTimeout);
                                });

                                container.appendChild(video);
                            }

                            function showFallbackMessage(url, container) {
                                var isWindows = navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Windows') !== -1;
                                var isLinux = navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Linux') !== -1 && navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Android') === -1;
                            
                                var div = document.createElement('div');
                                div.className = 'codec-fallback-container';

                                title.className = 'fallback-title';
                                title.textContent = 'Tu navegador no soporta los codecs necesarios';
                                div.appendChild(title);

                                subtitle.className = 'fallback-subtitle';

                                    var link = document.createElement('a');

                                    link.style.color = '#4db8ff';
                                    link.style.textDecoration = 'underline';
                                    link.textContent = 'Media Feature Pack';

Para solucionarlo debes usar un navegador alternativo como Chrome o instalar los codecs multimedia de tu distribucion (por ejemplo ');
                                    var code = document.createElement('code');
                                    code.style.color = '#fff';
                                    code.style.background = '#222';
                                    code.style.padding = '2px 6px';
                                    code.style.borderRadius = '4px';
                                    code.textContent = 'ubuntu-restricted-extras';
                                    var text2 = document.createTextNode(' en Ubuntu).');
                                    subtitle.appendChild(text1);
                                    subtitle.appendChild(code);
                                    subtitle.appendChild(text2);
                                } else {
                                    subtitle.textContent = 'Tu navegador no incluye el decodificador de audio AAC necesario para reproducir este canal.';
                                }
                            
                                container.innerHTML = '';
                                container.appendChild(div);
                            }

                            var _codecStatus = checkCodecSupport();
                            if (_codecStatus !== 'ok') {
                                console.warn('Codec check:', _codecStatus, '- Se usara reproductor fallback para M3U8');
                            }
                            // === FIN FALLBACK ===
                
                            // Listener para el botón de volumen, adjuntado una sola vez
                            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                                var volumeButton = document.getElementById('volume-button');
                                console.log('DOMContentLoaded fired. volumeButton:', volumeButton); // Depuración
                                if (volumeButton) {
                                    volumeButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
                                        if (activeClapprPlayer) {
                                            activeClapprPlayer.setVolume(85);
                                            activeClapprPlayer.configure({ mute: false });
                                            this.style.display = 'none';
                                        } else if (nativeVideo) {
                                            nativeVideo.muted = false;
                                            nativeVideo.volume = 0.85;
                                            this.style.display = 'none';
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
                            });
                        
                                var button1 = document.createElement('button');
                                button1.innerText = 'Emisión 1 (Panregional)';
                                button1.onclick = function() {
                                    if (players.length > 0) {
                                        players.forEach(player => player.destroy());
                                        players = [];
                                    }

                                    // === NO-REFERRER (anti-hotlink, p.ej. RTVE): reproductor nativo ===
                                    if (NEEDS_NOREFERRER) {
                                        createNativeHlsPlayer(decodedUrl, tabContent);
                                        activeClapprPlayer = null;
                                        var vbtn = document.getElementById('volume-button');
                                        if (vbtn) vbtn.style.display = 'block';
                                        var nbtns = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-buttons button');
                                        nbtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('active-button'));
                                        button1.classList.add('active-button');
                                        return;
                                    }
                            
                                    // === FALLBACK: Si no hay codec, usar reproductor alternativo ===
                                    if (_codecStatus !== 'ok') {
                                        createFallbackPlayer(decodedUrl, tabContent);
                                        activeClapprPlayer = null;
                                        var volumeButton = document.getElementById('volume-button');
                                        if (volumeButton) volumeButton.style.display = 'none';
                            
                                        var buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-buttons button');

                                    var hlsFallbackTimer = setTimeout(function() {

<script>
function toggleIosInstructions() {
    var container = document.getElementById("ios-pwa-instructions");
    if (container) {
        if (container.style.display === "none") {
            container.style.display = "block";
        } else {
            container.style.display = "none";
        }
    }
}
</script>

	<script type="speculationrules">
{"prefetch":[{"source":"document","where":{"and":[{"href_matches":"/*"},{"not":{"href_matches":["/wp-*.php","/wp-admin/*","/wp-content/uploads/*","/wp-content/*","/wp-content/plugins/*","/wp-content/themes/ATheme/*","/wp-content/themes/asap/*","/*\\?(.+)"]}},{"not":{"selector_matches":"a[rel~=\"nofollow\"]"}},{"not":{"selector_matches":".no-prefetch, .no-prefetch a"}}]},"eagerness":"conservative"}]}
</script>

    <template id="telo-popunder-template">
        <script src="https://supportiveinvoicevarnish.com/1e/85/ca/1e85ca4de7650381953ff21a5e5e4fb9.js"></script>    </template>

    <script id="teleonline-popunder-controller">
    (function() {
        'use strict';

        var CONFIG = {"delay":5,"maxPerSession":5,"maxPerDay":5,"cooldown":30,"mobileEnabled":false};

        var KEY_DAY       = 'telo_pop_day';
        var KEY_DAY_COUNT = 'telo_pop_daycount';
        var KEY_SESSION   = 'telo_pop_session';
        var KEY_LAST      = 'telo_pop_last';

        function isMobile() {
            return /Mobi|Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
        }

        if (isMobile() && !CONFIG.mobileEnabled) {
            return;
        }

        function lsGet(k) {
            try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch(e) { return null; }
        }
        function lsSet(k, v) {
            try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch(e) {}
        }
        function ssGet(k) {
            try { return sessionStorage.getItem(k); } catch(e) { return null; }
        }
        function ssSet(k, v) {
            try { sessionStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch(e) {}
        }

        function getToday() {
            return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        }

        function getDailyCount() {
            var day = lsGet(KEY_DAY);
            if (day !== getToday()) {
                lsSet(KEY_DAY, getToday());
                lsSet(KEY_DAY_COUNT, '0');
                return 0;
            }
            return parseInt(lsGet(KEY_DAY_COUNT) || '0', 10);
        }

        function incrementDaily() {
            lsSet(KEY_DAY, getToday());
            lsSet(KEY_DAY_COUNT, String(getDailyCount() + 1));
        }

        function getSessionCount() {
            return parseInt(ssGet(KEY_SESSION) || '0', 10);
        }

        function incrementSession() {
            ssSet(KEY_SESSION, String(getSessionCount() + 1));
        }

        function getLastTime() {
            return parseInt(lsGet(KEY_LAST) || '0', 10);
        }

        function setLastTime() {
            lsSet(KEY_LAST, String(Date.now()));
        }

        function canShow() {
            if (getSessionCount() >= CONFIG.maxPerSession) {
                return false;
            }
            if (getDailyCount() >= CONFIG.maxPerDay) {
                return false;
            }
            var last = getLastTime();
            if (last > 0) {
                var elapsed = (Date.now() - last) / 1000;
                if (elapsed < CONFIG.cooldown) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            return true;
        }

        var activated = false;

        function activate() {
            if (activated) return;
            if (!canShow()) return;

            activated = true;

            incrementSession();
            incrementDaily();
            setLastTime();

            var template = document.getElementById('telo-popunder-template');
            if (!template) return;

            var container = document.createElement('div');
            container.id = 'telo-popunder-active';
            container.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:-9999px;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;';
            document.body.appendChild(container);

            var clone = document.importNode(template.content, true);

            var scriptData = [];
            var scripts = clone.querySelectorAll('script');
            for (var i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {
                var s = scripts[i];
                scriptData.push({
                    src: s.getAttribute('src'),
                    text: s.textContent,
                    attrs: []
                });
                for (var j = 0; j < s.attributes.length; j++) {
                    var attr = s.attributes[j];
                    if (attr.name !== 'src') {
                        scriptData[i].attrs.push({name: attr.name, value: attr.value});
                    }
                }
                s.parentNode.removeChild(s);
            }

            container.appendChild(clone);

            for (var k = 0; k < scriptData.length; k++) {
                var data = scriptData[k];
                var newScript = document.createElement('script');

                for (var m = 0; m < data.attrs.length; m++) {
                    newScript.setAttribute(data.attrs[m].name, data.attrs[m].value);
                }

                if (data.src) {
                    newScript.src = data.src;
                    newScript.async = true;
                } else if (data.text) {
                    newScript.textContent = data.text;
                }

                document.body.appendChild(newScript);
            }

            template.parentNode.removeChild(template);
        }

        function tryActivate() {
            if (activated) return;

            if (getSessionCount() >= CONFIG.maxPerSession) return;
            if (getDailyCount() >= CONFIG.maxPerDay) return;

            var last = getLastTime();
            if (last > 0) {
                var elapsed = (Date.now() - last) / 1000;
                if (elapsed < CONFIG.cooldown) {
                    var remaining = (CONFIG.cooldown - elapsed) * 1000 + 100;
                    setTimeout(tryActivate, remaining);
                    return;
                }
            }

            activate();
        }

        function init() {
            if (CONFIG.delay === 0) {
                tryActivate();
            } else {
                setTimeout(tryActivate, CONFIG.delay * 1000);
            }
        }

        if (document.readyState === 'complete' || document.readyState === 'interactive') {
            init();
        } else {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
        }

    })();
    </script>

                    error.innerText = "Mínimo 3 caracteres"; error.style.display = 'block'; return;

                // Bloqueo estricto de palabras completas (no subcadenas)

                const isForbidden = hardBanned.some(b => wordMatch(val, b)) || bannedList.some(b => b !== "" && wordMatch(val, b));
                if (isForbidden) {
                    error.innerText = "Nombre no permitido"; error.style.display = 'block'; return;
                }

                // Unicidad DOM
                const screenNames = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#dmc-screen b')).map(b => b.innerText.replace(':','').trim().toLowerCase());

                    error.innerText = "Nombre en uso en esta sala"; error.style.display = 'block'; return;
                }

                localStorage.setItem('dmc_custom_name', cleanName);
                localStorage.setItem('dmc_name_locked', 'true');

                label.classList.add('locked');

            // Función de censura por palabras completas (word boundary)
            const dmcBannedList = ["maricones","maric\u00f3n","marica","maricon","polla","co\u00f1o","verga","xoxo","chupame la polla","puta","comerme la polla","poya","poia","comerme la             function dmcCensorMessage(text) {
                if (dmcBannedList.length === 0) return text;
                let result = text;
                dmcBannedList.forEach(word => {
                    const escaped = word.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
                    const regex = new RegExp('\\b(' + escaped + ')\\b', 'gi');
                    result = result.replace(regex, match => match[0] + '*'.repeat(match.length - 1));
                });
                return result;
            }

            window.dmcQuote = function(user) {
                const input = document.getElementById('dmc-msg-field');
                input.value = "@" + user + " ";
                input.focus();
                input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
            };

            window.dmcDeleteMsg = function(ts) {
                if (!confirm('¿Eliminar?')) return;
                const fd = new FormData();
                fd.append('action', 'dmc_delete_msg');
                fd.append('chat_id', 39392);
                fd.append('ts', ts);
                fetch('https://teleonline.org/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd }).then(() => { lastU = 0; runDmcCheck(); });
            };

            function toggleEmoteMode() {
                dmcEmoteMode = !dmcEmoteMode;
                document.getElementById('dmc-emotes-toggle').classList.toggle('active');
                lastU = 0; runDmcCheck();
            }

            function applyEmoteMask() {
                if (!dmcEmoteMode) return;
                const spans = document.querySelectorAll('#dmc-screen div > div > span');
                spans.forEach(span => {
                    const parent = span.closest('div');
                    const bTag = parent ? parent.querySelector('b') : null;
                    if (bTag && !bTag.innerText.includes('Admin')) {
                        let emojis = '';
                        for(let i=0; i < 3; i++) emojis += emotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * emotesList.length)];
                        span.textContent = emojis;
                    }
                });
            }

            function toggleChannelsPanel() {
                const panel = document.getElementById('dmc-channels-panel');
                panel.classList.toggle('dmc-panel-open');
            }

            function toggleDMC() {
                const wrapper = document.getElementById('dmc-wrapper');
                const isClosed = wrapper.classList.toggle('dmc-closed');
                // Cerrar panel de canales si está abierto
                const chPanel = document.getElementById('dmc-channels-panel');
                if (chPanel) chPanel.classList.remove('dmc-panel-open');

                if (!isClosed) {
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                        const media = document.querySelector('video, iframe[src*="youtube"], iframe[src*="vimeo"], .clappr-style video, #pwp-video-player-container video');
                        if (media && !originalMediaElement) {
                            originalMediaElement = media; originalMediaParent = media.parentNode;
                            document.getElementById('dmc-media-outer').appendChild(media);
                        }
                    }
                    document.getElementById('dmc-msg-field').focus(); runDmcCheck();
                } else {
                    if (originalMediaElement && originalMediaParent) originalMediaParent.appendChild(originalMediaElement);
                    originalMediaElement = null;
                    if (dmcPollingTimer) { clearTimeout(dmcPollingTimer); dmcPollingTimer = null; }
                }
            }

            async function runDmcCheck() {
                if (document.getElementById('dmc-wrapper').classList.contains('dmc-closed')) return;
                try {
                    const r = await fetch('https://teleonline.org/wp-content/plugins/configuracion-tv/chat/status.php?post_id=39392&t=' + Date.now());
                    const t = await r.text();
                    if (t !== lastU) {
                        lastU = t;
                        const m = await fetch('https://teleonline.org/wp-content/plugins/configuracion-tv/chat/leer-chat.php?post_id=39392&adm=&t=' + Date.now());
                        document.getElementById('dmc-screen').innerHTML = await m.text();
                        if (dmcEmoteMode) applyEmoteMask();
                        document.getElementById('dmc-screen').scrollTop = document.getElementById('dmc-screen').scrollHeight;
                    }
                } catch (e) {}
                dmcPollingTimer = setTimeout(runDmcCheck, 4000);
            }

            document.getElementById('dmc-input-form').onsubmit = async (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (isLocked) return;
                const input = document.getElementById('dmc-msg-field'), btn = document.getElementById('dmc-send-btn');
                const msg = input.value.trim();
                if (!msg) return;

                const now = Date.now();
                msgHistory = msgHistory.filter(ts => now - ts < 6000);
                msgHistory.push(now);

                input.value = ''; document.getElementById('dmc-char-count').innerText = "0 / 140";
                
                const fd = new FormData();
                fd.append('post_id', 39392); fd.append('msg', dmcCensorMessage(msg)); fd.append('custom_name', label.innerText); 
                fd.append('adm', ''); fd.append('log', '');

                try {
                    await fetch('https://teleonline.org/wp-content/plugins/configuracion-tv/chat/post-comentario.php', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
                    if (msgHistory.length >= 3) {
                        isLocked = true; input.disabled = true; btn.disabled = true;
                        let cooldown = 30;
                        const originalLabelText = label.innerText;
                        const timer = setInterval(() => {
                            label.innerText = "Spam: " + cooldown + "s";
                            if (cooldown-- <= 0) {
                                clearInterval(timer); isLocked = false; input.disabled = false; btn.disabled = false;
                                label.innerText = originalLabelText;
                            }
                        }, 1000);
                    }
                    lastU = 0; runDmcCheck();
                } catch (e) {}
            };

        <script>
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                const userAgent = navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
                const banner = document.getElementById('teleonline-donation-banner');

    (function() {
      'use strict';
      
      if (!('modelContext' in navigator) || typeof navigator.modelContext.provideContext !== 'function') {
        console.log('[WebMCP] No soportado');
        return;
      }
      
      window.addEventListener('load', function() {
        setTimeout(function() {
          try {
            navigator.modelContext.provideContext({
              tools: [
                {
                  name: "search_articles",
                  description: "Buscar artículos en Teleonline.org",
                  inputSchema: {
                    type: "object",
                    properties: {
                      query: { type: "string", description: "Término de búsqueda" }
                    },
                    required: ["query"]
                  },
                  execute: async function(input) {
                    const url = "https://teleonline.org/wp-json/wp/v2/posts?search=" + encodeURIComponent(input.query) + "&per_page=10";
                    try {
                      const response = await fetch(url);
                      const data = await response.json();
                      return {
                        success: true,
                        count: data.length,
                        results: data.map(function(post) {
                          return {
                            title: post.title.rendered,
                            url: post.link,
                            excerpt: post.excerpt.rendered.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').substring(0, 200)
                          };
                        })
                      };
                    } catch (e) {
                      return { success: false, error: e.message };
                    }
                  }
                },
                {
                  name: "get_tv_guide",
                  description: "Abrir guía de programación TV",
                  inputSchema: { type: "object", properties: {} },
                  execute: async function() {
                    window.open("https://teleonline.org/guia-tv/", "_blank");
                    return { success: true, url: "https://teleonline.org/guia-tv/" };
                  }
                },
                {
                  name: "list_categories",
                  description: "Listar categorías de contenido",
                  inputSchema: { type: "object", properties: {} },
                  execute: async function() {
                    try {
                      const response = await fetch("https://teleonline.org/wp-json/wp/v2/categories?per_page=50");
                      const data = await response.json();
                      return {
                        success: true,
                        categories: data.map(function(c) {
                          return { id: c.id, name: c.name, slug: c.slug, count: c.count };
                        })
                      };
                    } catch (e) {
                      return { success: false, error: e.message };
                    }
                  }
                },
                {
                  name: "get_latest_posts",
                  description: "Obtener últimos artículos publicados",
                  inputSchema: {
                    type: "object",
                    properties: {
                      limit: { type: "integer", description: "Número de artículos (1-20)", default: 10 }
                    }
                  },
                  execute: async function(input) {
                    const limit = (input && input.limit) ? input.limit : 10;
                    try {
                      const response = await fetch("https://teleonline.org/wp-json/wp/v2/posts?per_page=" + limit);
                      const data = await response.json();
                      return {
                        success: true,
                        posts: data.map(function(p) {
                          return {
                            title: p.title.rendered,
                            url: p.link,
                            date: p.date
                          };
                        })
                      };
                    } catch (e) {
                      return { success: false, error: e.message };
                    }
                  }
                },
                {
                  name: "get_site_info",
                  description: "Información de Teleonline.org",
                  inputSchema: { type: "object", properties: {} },
                  execute: async function() {
                    return {
                      success: true,
                      name: "Teleonline",
                      url: "https://teleonline.org/",
                      description: "Portal televisión online - Guía TV y contenido",
                      api: "https://teleonline.org/wp-json/wp/v2"
                    };
                  }
                }
              ]
            });
            console.log('[WebMCP] 5 herramientas registradas');
          } catch (e) {
            console.error('[WebMCP] Error:', e);
          }
        }, 500);
      });
    })();
    </script>
